[FSA] Extend expiry of File System Access histograms

These File System Access permission and FileSystemObserver histograms
expired on 2026-08-31, and ObserverUsageQuotaExceeded expires on
2026-11-15. The recording code is still in place and the permission
and observer data still inform FSA work, so extend them to 2027-07-11.

The revived histograms expired more than a month ago, so each summary
now carries the expiry warning that tools/metrics/histograms/README.md
asks for. ObserverUsageQuotaExceeded has not expired and needs none.

FilePathWatcherCallbackError recorded almost no samples in the past
year, so remove it and its recording code instead of extending it.

Fixed: 536899274, 570000050, 472308420
Test: python3 tools/metrics/histograms/validate_format.py
Test: autoninja -C out/Default file_path_watcher_{inotify,histogram}.cc^

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in WaylandFrameManager::HandleFrameCallback

In WaylandFrameManager::HandleFrameCallback, the frame callback received
from Wayland might not match the submitted frame callback in certain
scenarios, resulting in a crash in release builds when CHECK was used.

This change reverts the CHECK macro back to DCHECK with an explanatory
TODO comment according to the CHECK conversion exclusion guidelines.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/b2c266a088400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in PredictionMetricsHandler::AddRealEvent

Revert CHECK back to DCHECK in PredictionMetricsHandler::AddRealEvent
when checking if event_exists, as this CHECK can be triggered in certain
delegated ink trail scenarios. Annotate it with a CHECK-exclusion
comment until it can be safely converted.

Revert CHECK_GT back to DCHECK_GT in
OSExchangeDataProviderWin::GetPickledData

A CHECK_GT was recently introduced to verify that c_data size is greater
than zero when retrieving pickled data in OSExchangeDataProviderWin.
However, this check can be triggered in production when c_data size is
0.

This change reverts the CHECK_GT back to DCHECK_GT with a CHECK-
exclusion annotation until we are confident it will not be triggered.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/acf3d71c08400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Revert CHECK for non-empty GetName() to DCHECK in ComputeAttributes

The CHECK for non-empty name when NameFrom is kContents in
AXPlatformNodeBase::ComputeAttributes was causing crashes in release
builds when nodes with kContents had empty names. Reverting this check
to a DCHECK resolves the crash until the underlying condition can be
fully verified.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/5c770ea088400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

This caused crashes when the SQL statement validation failed.

This change reverts the CHECK macro back to DCHECK in SaveNodeChildren
to avoid crashes in non-DCHECK builds while investigating the root
cause.

tree
5e10966197b329de0af062a24bfe087ae9e574a1
parent
105e8b2c15d0c5e915e4ff19a42b15517dd29064 [ diff ] 
Layout: Extend fast script segmentation to Latin Extended and punctuation

InlineNode::SegmentScriptRuns() skips RunSegmenter and bidi detection
only if text_content has no 16-bit characters other than U+FFFC. A
single curly quote, dash or ellipsis, or a letter such as Œ, sends the
whole paragraph through the slow path.

Latin Extended-A/B (U+0100-U+024F) and General Punctuation
U+2010-U+2027 are Latin or Common script, not emoji, and not RTL, so
they segment the same way as Latin-1. Exempt them too.

This reduces Speedometer3 Editor-TipTap time by 0.6-1.1% on Pinpoint
(win-11-perf, mac-m1_mini_2020-perf) without regressing the total score.

tree
5e318acea407fd731bd91dbbe90c374274f2362c
parent
5c95f0112b1465cf35b48a3b37d3bdcc0a75091a [ diff ] 
Prewarm Shared Brotli dictionary metadata on SearchEnginePreconnect

When preconnecting to the Default Search Engine in SearchEnginePreconnector,
if the feature kSearchEnginePrewarmDictionaryOnPreconnect is enabled,
trigger early prewarming of dictionary metadata via
PreloadSharedDictionaryInfoForDocument.

Introduces a CleanupMode feature param with 3 experimental arms:
1. "events_only": Relies solely on connection closure / failure events
   (OnSessionClosed and OnConnectionFailed).
2. "timer_only": Relies solely on the fallback timer (default 30s).
3. "hybrid" (default): Cleans up on connection events or when the fallback
   timer fires, whichever comes first.

Also attaches a Mojo disconnect handler to PreloadedSharedDictionaryInfoHandle
to immediately release pinned dictionary handles if the Network Service
drops them on memory pressure or unexpected disconnect.

Browser tests are added in search_engine_preconnector_browsertest.cc
covering dictionary prewarming on start, suggest-disabled gating, HTTP
DSE gating, early cleanup on session closed and connection failure,
timer-only persistence, and events-only mode.

TAG=agy
CONV=a344fdf5-f3fc-4e37-8b81-c33a206ef0ff

a11y: Stop including c/c/extensions/extension_constants.h in e2e tests

The extension IDs referenced from the generated C++ code of the
accessibility E2E tests are all defined in
ash/constants/ash_extension_constants.h or extensions/common/constants.h.
Include them directly instead of
chrome/common/extensions/extension_constants.h.

Update the deps of the js2gtest targets accordingly: add //ash/constants
and //extensions/common, and remove //chrome/common, which is no longer
needed except for braille_ime_extjs_tests (chrome/common/chrome_paths.h).

aeb8ad6c6aa1341cd1e2eeb73d1d9572f161041a
parent
c39fcc5f266d26b1693bd1ad0bd51666c4fb9b66 [ diff ] 
Propagate NetworkIsolationPartition for SharedWorker network requests

Previously, `SharedWorkerHost::GetNetworkIsolationPartition()` was only
applied when computing `NetworkIsolationKey` and
`NetworkAnonymizationKey` via a local helper, while
`SharedWorkerHost::ComputeIsolationInfoForNetworkRequest()` omitted the
partition when reconstructing `net::IsolationInfo` with a null
`SiteForCookies`. Additionally, `SharedWorkerServiceImpl::CreateWorker()`
passed `worker_storage_key().ToPartialNetIsolationInfo()` directly for
main script fetches instead of using
`ComputeIsolationInfoForNetworkRequest()`.

Update `SharedWorkerHost::ComputeIsolationInfoForNetworkRequest()` to
include `GetNetworkIsolationPartition()` and unify
`GetNetworkIsolationKey()`, `GetNetworkAnonymizationKey()`, and main
script fetches in `SharedWorkerServiceImpl::CreateWorker()` to use it.
Also update `net::URLRequest::Start()` and
`net::CookiePartitionKey::FromNetworkIsolationKey()` to permit
`kSharedWorkerSameSiteCookiesNone` for credentialed requests with a null
`SiteForCookies`.

tree
ba2c85877ef7609730eefd5d2a665929e0403dea
parent
c312a728d364de1ec2be8cfcb740502aeff94316 [ diff ] 
[ruby] Wrap <ruby> after a preserved space instead of overflowing

With `white-space: pre-wrap` or `break-spaces`, a <ruby> that doesn't
fit after a space stays on the line and overflows the container. A
plain word, or the same ruby with `white-space: normal`, wraps at the
space.

Each ruby column starts with a bidi isolate control character.
CanBreakAfter() already skips it, but it asks the break iterator about
the position after the control character, and the line breaking rules
don't allow a break there. This CL removes the control characters from
the text and asks about the position right after the space, the same
way CanBreakAfterRubyColumn() does.

Text that ends a ruby column keeps the old behavior. Otherwise a
collapsible space between `</rt>` and `</ruby>` gains a break
opportunity, which breaks css-multicol/multicol-fill-balance-031.html.

The change is behind the RubySoftWrapAfterPreservedSpace runtime flag.

Test: blink_unittests --gtest_filter=LineBreakerTest.Ruby*
Test: external/wpt/css/css-ruby/line-break-around-ruby-preserved-space.html

FSA: CHECK that CreateDirectoryHandle() gets a valid URL

The DCHECK was converted to a CHECK once, and demoted back because it
failed in the field (crash/c951228970400000), and this should be fixed
this by https://crrev.com/c/8637410, skipping the entries of such a
directory, and by making GetChildURL() fail for them.

Convert it back to a CHECK. Use NotFatalUntil so that any remaining
callers show up in crash reports before the CHECK becomes fatal.

f081604be0cf9661b9a9cff182b023065c7c1079 [ diff ] 
base: Use a fold expression to implement VariantIndexOfType()

That's much faster to compile than constant-evaluating a variant<Ts...>
constructor.

variant_util.h itself is included over 11k TUs.

blink::MultiToken::IndexOf() uses VariantIndexOfType(), and
tokens_mojom_traits.h calls IndexOf() 22 times in inline functions and
is included in > 2k TUs.

Subset of a 'chrome' build with default args for Linux x64 (debug,
component, clang modules), sum over 60 random TUs that include:

  tokens_mojom_traits.h (2345 in chrome):
                  before     after
  CPU time        249.7 s    244.5 s    -2.10%
  instructions    2458.4e9   2402.7e9   -2.27%
  mean max RSS    891 MB     867 MB     -2.65%

  variant_util.h (11806 in chrome):
                  before     after
  CPU time        127.3 s    126.4 s    -0.70%
  instructions    1286.8e9   1280.2e9   -0.51%
  mean max RSS    509 MB     507 MB     -0.33%

Estimated to save about 0.3% of the compile time of the whole build.

No behavior change (except that diagnostics change a bit).

79a305fd49966aada9c7c777d142f15017ed5dd8 [ diff ] 
[webium] Prerender under the shared renderer and evict on pressure

Design: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1lzWRQwj2u4QItO-Wd3BbhoJbvc7dIxLFBpZeGumxEmw

Two follow-ups to TopChromeWebUIPrerendering
(https://crrev.com/c/8690793), both part of Phase 1:

1. Shared renderer: InitialWebUIProfileServiceFactory created no
   service under TopChromeWebUISharedRenderer, so prerendering had no
   effect there. It now creates one when prerendering is enabled. Each
   spare is still built in TopChromeWebUIProfile for its user profile,
   and only that profile's windows adopt it.
2. Eviction: memory pressure only skipped building a spare, and a spare
   that already existed stayed. The new
   TopChromeWebUIPrerenderingEvictMemoryPressure param ("never" by
   default, "moderate", or "critical") makes WebUIContentsPrerenderer
   drop its spare at or below that level and skip refills there too, so
   the spare stays dropped while the pressure lasts.
   TopChromeWebUIPrerenderingMemoryPressure, which only skips refills,
   is renamed to TopChromeWebUIPrerenderingSkipMemoryPressure for
   parity; no config uses the old name yet.

The prerenderer only compares the memory limit with these levels and
resizes nothing, so its memory consumer declares no memory limit
support. Memory Saver's limit cap, which is meant for resizable caches,
then no longer applies to it.

Bug: b:573441656
Test: out/Default/unit_tests --gtest_filter='WebUIContentsPrerender*:InitialWebUIProfileService*'
Test: out/Default/browser_tests --gtest_filter='*InitialWebUIProfilePrewarm*:*SharedRenderer*'

Reviewed-by: Thomas Lukaszewicz <tluk@chromium.org>

Revert CHECK_LE in ui::BitSet32::value_for_bit back to DCHECK_LE

Converting the bounds check in ui::BitSet32::value_for_bit to a CHECK
causes crashes when out-of-bounds bit indices are passed. Revert the
CHECK_LE macro back to DCHECK_LE and add an exclusion TODO comment.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/3885baa088400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in FindDisplayWithBiggestIntersection

When displays is empty, FindDisplayWithBiggestIntersection triggers a
CHECK. Convert it back to a DCHECK to avoid crashes until we are

tree
7a6ae8f0eeba2d5a46241a3545745b9be38fb156
parent
754770d39831f3b9302f78d60840a0c431df60ff [ diff ] 
mahi: Bound and compress MahiCacheManager page cache

MahiCacheManager kept extracted page text, summaries, Q&A, and
favicons in an unbounded std::map for 7 days.

- Bound the cache with a base::LRUCache (20 entries) and expire
  entries after 30 minutes. The cache only helps when Help me read is
  used again on a recently used page. Expired entries are removed on
  access, so no timer is needed.
- Store the extracted page content zstd-compressed (level 1) in
  MahiData. The content can be several MB (e.g. PDFs) and is only
  read on a summary/Q&A request, so decompressing on access is cheap.
- Release MahiManagerImpl's panel state and discard pending requests
  when the Mahi panel closes.
- Clear the global page cache and discard pending requests when the
  active user session changes so cached summaries and content do not
  leak across Multi-Sign-In users.

UMA (ChromeOS.Mahi.CacheStateOnAccess, ChromeOS Stable, 30 days) with
the 7-day cache: 76% no hit, 20% content hit (skips local extraction
only), 5% summary hit (skips the server request). Long retention adds
little.

CONV=6d264d6a-5054-4cf4-8eeb-2d561d8124cc

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in ClearWindowPropertyStore.

Revert the CHECK macro in ClearWindowPropertyStore back to a DCHECK. The
condition verifying that property count is zero after clearing can fail
due to non-fatal property store residual keys, causing crashes in
release builds. Reverting back to DCHECK prevents release crashes while
preserving the check for debug builds.

Convert CHECK in BaseShellDialogImpl::BeginRun to DCHECK

In BaseShellDialogImpl::BeginRun, checking that a dialog is not already
running for the given owner was converted to a fatal CHECK. This causes
crashes in release builds when a dialog is triggered while another is
running for the same owner.

This CL reverts the CHECK back to a DCHECK to avoid crashing in release
builds.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/4bdfa88c08400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

[Glic] Support Android background tab prep in transport handler

When GlicExperimentalTriggering requests arrive over the browser
actuator transport channel on Android with kGlicBackgroundTriggering
enabled, trigger_actuation_request messages need to wait for an
offscreen background tab before routing to the coordinator. Move the
Android background tab preparation and BackgroundActuationObserver
lifecycle management from GlicExperimentalTriggeringMessageHandler into
GlicExperimentalTriggeringCoordinator::OnProtoMessage so both the FCM
and transport handlers share a single implementation.

[dns] Keep HostResolverManager::Job alive to cache DNS results

891b98cbc1bc2d5ba18302f5d8decab508c90e3e

tree
1787d5c2aac1caf954fac8357d08e96604947455
parent
43174e0b0bdfab8bdf46afdcf468e03f0f562fef [ diff ] 
[dns] Keep HostResolverManager::Job alive to cache DNS results

With intermediate DNS results, consumers such as QUIC AsyncDnsJob often
succeed and cancel their request while some DNS responses are still in
flight. The HostResolverManager::Job is then cancelled and nothing is
written to HostCache.

Keep such a HostResolverManager::Job running without requests if its
DnsTask has received at least one response and needs to send no more
queries, so that the final results are cached. It never falls back to
other tasks and gives up after a cap. The Happy Eyeballs v3 draft
recommends processing DNS replies for 1s after a connection succeeds,
but the default cap is 2s: Net.Dns.ResolveTimeDiff2.* on Android shows
a peak around 1s from classic DNS retransmitting after 1s.

Guarded by the new kKeepHostResolverJobAliveForCache feature, disabled
by default, to be rolled out via Finch. kAsyncDnsQuicJob also enables it
via a new param (default true), so that the AsyncDnsQuicJob study gets
it without taking over the feature from other studies.

32b38a0b2b0ff18a75c8e6686ecd872ca99266ee
parent
1636fd9f6b0406297425b1887d89d3247d3425f7 [ diff ] 
sandbox/linux: Allow direct socket syscalls on i386 in seccomp-bpf

Linux added direct socket system calls on i386 in kernel 4.3 (such as
__NR_recvmsg = 372, __NR_recvfrom = 371, __NR_socket = 359, etc.).
While __NR_sendmsg and __NR_sendto were previously updated to include
__i386__, __NR_recvmsg and __NR_recvfrom were omitted, causing processes
(such as 32-bit x86 Android WebView renderers) to trap with SIGSYS on
__NR_recvmsg when libc transitions off legacy multiplexed socketcall(2).

Update sandbox/linux/seccomp-bpf-helpers to:
- Include defined(__i386__) in SyscallSets::IsAllowedGeneralIo() for
  __NR_recvfrom and __NR_recvmsg.
- Include defined(__i386__) in SyscallSets::IsDeniedGetOrModifySocket()
  so disallowed socket syscalls fail gracefully with -EPERM instead of
  triggering CrashSIGSYS (guarding __NR_accept with #if defined(__NR_accept)
  as direct accept() does not exist on x86-32, which only has accept4).
- Include defined(__i386__) in SyscallSets::IsNetworkSocketInformation()
  and update syscall_sets.h and baseline_policy.cc accordingly.

ASH Refactoring: Move device settings strings to ash (Part 2)

Inputs page UI strings and search tags for OS Settings (input methods,
spell check, custom dictionary, and language-specific IME options) were
defined under //chrome/app/, coupling inputs_section.cc to
//chrome/grit/generated_resources.h.

from os_settings_search_tag_strings.grdp used by inputs_section.cc
(along with their 153 screenshot hashes) to ash_settings_strings.grd and
ash_settings_search_tag_strings.grdp, and drop generated_resources and
+chrome/grit from pages/device/.

[segmentation] Re-enable `BackgroundRecorderTest.Recording`

Re-enable `BackgroundRecorderTest.Recording` in `components_unittests`
with mock time to avoid test suite slowdown.

`BackgroundRecorderTest.Recording` was disabled on iOS in
https://crrev.com/c/5112863 (tracked in
https://issues.chromium.org/issues/299529800) because waiting on
`BackgroundUmaRecorder`'s 5-second delayed flush task with real time
slowed down `components_unittests`.

Extract a shared `StatsBaseTest` base fixture for `StatsTest` and
`BackgroundRecorderTest` that configures `base::test::TaskEnvironment`
with `TimeSource::MOCK_TIME` and resets
`BackgroundUmaRecorder::GetInstance()` in `TearDown()`. This eliminates
the real-time delay while preserving background recording verification.

This CL re-enables `BackgroundRecorderTest.Recording` in
`components_unittests`. Verified on iOS and Linux.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

a61b25efde161caf60e6ff660f5254893ca505b1
parent
b731f205144b7c434e1f8c52f9a26a6de60aef68 [ diff ] 
fuzzilli: Disable TopChromeWebUISharedRenderer in js_in_process_fuzzer

The field trial testing config now enables TopChromeWebUISharedRenderer
[1], which moves the top-chrome WebUIs, such as the WebUI toolbar, to
their own profile with its own storage partition [2].
js_in_process_fuzzer runs Chrome in single-process mode, where all
pages share one renderer process regardless of their profile, so
opening the first tab at startup fails the storage partition CHECK in
RenderProcessHostImpl::GetProcessHostForSiteInstance() [3], and the
fuzzer never starts.

Disable the feature TopChromeWebUISharedRenderer.

[1]: https://crrev.com/c/8666468
[2]: https://crsrc.org/c/chrome/browser/ui/webui/top_chrome/top_chrome_webui_profile.cc;l=41;drc=8acadd2f4eda7b7edf370f7fae38df2a733ce108
[3]: https://crsrc.org/c/content/browser/renderer_host/render_process_host_impl.cc;l=5449;drc=50c1703e5b940a07cd76046bf85cd66ec13b171f

Mock GetWindow() in SkillRecordingServiceTest's TestBrowserInstance

On official Windows Chrome builds (is_chrome_branded = true),
GlobalFeatures registers InstallerDownloaderActiveBrowserWindowTracker
as a GlobalBrowserCollection observer. When the active mock browser
closes at the end of SkillRecordingServiceTest.IgnoresClicksFromOtherProfiles,
OnBrowserClosed calls GlobalBrowserCollection::GetActiveBrowser(), which
dereferences browser->GetWindow()->IsActive(). Because
TestBrowserInstance did not configure MockBrowserWindowInterface's
GetWindow(), it returned nullptr and crashed.

https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Aunit_tests%21gtest%3A%3ASkillRecordingServiceTest%23IgnoresClicksFromOtherProfiles

Back TestBrowserInstance with a NiceMock<ui::MockBaseWindow> returned by
GetWindow(), matching ContextHubTabProviderDesktopTest.

Bug: None

1579291637fc554e4055f136eff7f6de360d6a3e
parent
faf9749ba4e39d13c2b6748fdbf7a5915c306a8e [ diff ] 
context_hub: Send stored preferences with GROUPING requests

`GenerateTabGroups()` now populates
`ContextHubRequest.auto_tab_groups_preferences` (field 7) with the
user's stored Auto Tab Groups preferences document. The field is only
set when a document is actually stored: the built-in defaults describe
the server's own behaviour and are never sent, so requests from users
who have not edited anything are byte-identical to today. Preferences
travel in their own field and never leak into `user_command`.

Safe against an old server (unknown field ignored); the server reads the
field under a `{{if .GetAutoTabGroupsPreferences}}` block in the
grouping prompt with precedence constraints > user command >
preferences.

Bug: 572845099
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter='ContextHubServiceTest.GroupTabs_Sends*'

49150904cf2cc3c6e988ad673f16986939a149ee [ diff ] 
ui: Use gin's compile-time SetMethod<&T::Method>() form

Mechanical change: .SetMethod("x", &T::X) becomes .SetMethod<&T::X>("x"),
and likewise for SetProperty() and SetLazyDataProperty(). The template
form added in https://crrev.com/c/8461371 calls the target directly
instead of through a heap-allocated base::RepeatingCallback holder on
every JS -> C++ call. Bound callbacks and lambdas keep the value form.

No behaviour change.

tree
99bd91d43d2a91f51ae2eca8cb0d7911764bbe1c
parent
3b6c9f6a3e31aa1bd4484032aa5ff79d4bec9200 [ diff ] 
[page_load_metrics] Only record NTP FCP and LCP for foreground loads

This CL guards NewTabPage.LoadTime.FirstContentfulPaint2 and
NewTabPage.LoadTime.LargestContentfulPaint in
NewTabPagePageLoadMetricsObserver with
page_load_metrics::WasStartedInForegroundOptionalEventInForeground().

Previously, NewTabPagePageLoadMetricsObserver recorded FCP and LCP
unconditionally, unlike UmaPageLoadMetricsObserver and
NewTabPageInitiatedPageLoadMetricsObserver. When a New Tab Page was
opened or restored in a background tab and activated minutes later,
FCP and LCP recorded the total elapsed time since navigation start,
skewing high percentiles into the 5-10 minute range.

Bug: 582956097

ef42a76f2db1a48efca1bf002239c00d12c6f80a
parent
3838aedec523d50e7e08b8bbd4899c3b6ac11adf [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Add metrics for outgoing page disposal on Omnibox navigation

To evaluate the potential impact and opportunity size of Delayed Frame
Unload on Omnibox navigations (specifically Search and direct URL input),
this CL adds metrics tracking the lifecycle outcome of the outgoing page
being navigated away from.

The metrics record whether the previous document entered the
Back/Forward Cache, was unloaded cross-process or same-process, or
was an initial empty document / about:blank.

This establishes fleet-wide ground truth on what fraction of Omnibox
navigations undergo unload and document destruction contention at commit
time versus entering BFCache.

Bug: 457812977

5cc987aafbc87becc2287e3f7175f0c04d8fb60b [ diff ] 
Cursor: Draw cursors at the device scale factor

`wm::CursorLoader` drew cursors at the display's resource scale, 1x or
2x, instead of its device scale factor. The cursor assets are Lottie
vector images now, so they can be drawn at any scale. This change draws
cursors at the DSF and removes the resource scale.

Doc: http://goto.google.com/cursor-scales
Bug: 565470046

tree
3fd16420a69310448c0ddb819fac83117b06fd24
parent
eb05ec46586b4b8ef4d47fc92476f66dfa825b1f [ diff ] 
[Webium] Pass profile-dependent organizer panel values to each frame

A WebUIDataSource belongs to a BrowserContext, so in
TopChromeWebUIProfile, it is shared by the windows of all profiles. It
must not have values that depend on the profile of a window.

OrganizerPanelUI::GetProfileLoadTimeData() returns the panel's values
that depend on the profile: isUserSignedIn and isIncognitoMode. They are
added to the data source as before, unless the data source is shared.
Then, WebUIRenderFrameCreated() passes them to each frame instead, and
the panel adds them to loadTimeData before creating its elements, like
the omnibox popup (crrev.com/c/8622258).

SetWebUIProperty() needs BindingsPolicyValue::kWebUi, so enable
chrome.send for the panel only when the data source is shared, like the
WebUI toolbar (crrev.com/c/8694717). The panel doesn't send any message
with chrome.send().

Also move IsWebUIDataSourceShared() from the omnibox popup to
top_chrome_webui_profile.h, so that both WebUIs use it.

A follow-up CL moves passing the values to each frame to
TopChromeWebUIController, since the omnibox popup does the same and
other Top Chrome WebUIs will.

Nothing changes unless the panel is in TopChromeWebUIProfile, which a
follow-up CL does.

fc896ec102f8da9c32a2a032f172d57697f6591c

tree
0b38765e50c4c6ebd81f25da5f6a99297e41241a
parent
eca769094811b59b81daa8d22bfe3f4514c60ea1 [ diff ] 
Keep the children collection out of NodeListsNodeData's hash map

ContainerNode::children() stored its HTMLCollection in
NodeListsNodeData::atomic_name_caches_, a hash map whose smallest table
has 8 buckets of 24 bytes on 64-bit. Store it in a dedicated Member
instead, so a node whose only cached collection is children no longer
allocates that 192-byte table, and children() no longer does a hash
table lookup on every call.

The Member fills existing padding on 64-bit, so
sizeof(NodeListsNodeData) is unchanged there (64 bytes). On 32-bit it
grows by 4 bytes.

Reland "Add a 3pp package for the snap runtime libraries"

This is a reland of commit 7b636458c534e1bdb29e76e2e3cab2270595e819.

Fix since original CL (PS1 -> PS2):
The CI linux-presubmit and win-presubmit builders invoke
presubmit_support.py with --all --no_diffs, which marks every tracked
file as affected with OldContents() == NewContents(). Once the original
CL landed, CheckVersionBumped in
//chrome/installer/linux/snap/runtime/PRESUBMIT.py saw 3pp/* files as
affected without a version change in runtime.lock.json and failed.

Skip CheckVersionBumped when input_api.no_diffs is true, and add
PRESUBMIT_test.py covering --no_diffs and normal edits.

Original change's description:
> Add a 3pp package for the snap runtime libraries
>
> The snap package bundles a few Ubuntu 26.04 libraries that the core26
> base does not provide (NSS, libsecret, opensc-pkcs11, Qt 6). Today
> snapcraft fetches them as stage-packages, which needs snapcraft and
> snapd on the builders and is not pinned.
>
> Add //chrome/installer/linux/snap/runtime/3pp, which the
> 3pp-linux-amd64-packager builder uploads to CIPD as
> chromium/chrome/installer/linux/snap/runtime/linux-amd64:
> - update.py (developer tool) resolves the packages in config.json with
>   apt-get at a snapshot.ubuntu.com timestamp. apt runs in a private
>   root with the core26 base packages marked as installed, checks the
>   archive signature against the Ubuntu archive signing key (2018) and
>   downloads the .debs. update.py records their URLs, sizes and SHA256s
>   in runtime.lock.json, along with the files the gnome extension's
>   cleanup parts would remove (libraries provided by the content snaps).
> - build.py only reads runtime.lock.json: it downloads the pinned .debs,
>   checks their SHA256s, extracts them and applies the prime filters of
>   the snapcraft parts and the cleanup list.
>
> The man page part (man-db, groff) is not included. The DEPS entry
> (gated on a new checkout_snap_runtime gclient var, checked out to
> runtime/linux-amd64) and the build.py changes follow once the builder
> has uploaded a version.
>
> Verified locally: at snapshot 20261103T000000Z, update.py resolves the
> same packages as snapcraft's stage-packages in an Ubuntu 26.04 VM, except for the empty transitional package lsb-base (provided
> by sysvinit-utils in core26). The installed files match snapcraft's
> prime directory except for the man page part and one pkg-config file
> that snapcraft rewrites.
>
> Bug: 581012931
> Change-Id: I914ab86d7132a3c100db903494fc8ccc4a329e66
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8711076

Reserve capacity in ShapeResult::IndividualCharacterRanges

Pre-allocates the vector capacity based on NumCharacters() to avoid
reallocations when populating character ranges.

It was reported as the largest unreserved vector in the Blink codebase,
at thousands to >50,000+ chars on long <pre> / code / CJK text blocks
during selection, IME, and DevTools highlight queries (AddRunInfoRanges
pushes run->num_characters_ entries per run).

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
[Webium] Don't use TopChromeWebUIProfile in single-process mode
📋 Metadata
commit
118a270a21f34d71a46521eb4338c9d10356008f
[ log ]
author
Anna Sato <annasato@chromium.org>
Mon Nov 09 00:22:08 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Mon Nov 09 00:22:08 2026
tree
ddc76bc0d2fe4cd1a83b47e765c1eb1ca62483d1
parent
7ac45ca40a1bd4e87a6735ea461c7a555bd454e7 [ diff ] 
[Webium] Don't use TopChromeWebUIProfile in single-process mode

With --single-process, all frames run in the only renderer process,
which RenderProcessHostImpl reuses for every SiteInstance without
checking its BrowserContext. With kTopChromeWebUISharedRenderer, the
WebUI toolbar is created in TopChromeWebUIProfile, which has its own
storage partition, so Chrome crashes at startup on the CHECK that the
process is in the same storage partition. In-process fuzzers run in
single-process mode, and the testing config enables the feature.

Add webui::ShouldUseTopChromeWebUIProfile(), which is false when
renderers run in the browser process, and use it wherever
kTopChromeWebUISharedRenderer decides whether to use
TopChromeWebUIProfile: creating the profile, choosing the profile for
Top Chrome WebUIs, and skipping the toolbar prewarming service in user
profiles.

Bug: b:582738849

2c3473f4e83a2c20127c2ced5d327e084b14dc0c
parent
fcd623bff51db452f111a43d15173c52da6836f3 [ diff ] 
[SUIT] Don't dedupe matches whose templates do different things

Deduping keys on the stripped destination URL, which drops search
parameters like udm and never contains a template's fusebox action. So
matches that do different things on click, e.g. an AI Mode or tool
suggestion and a plain search for the same query, could dedupe, and the
best match could show a duplicate's presentation (e.g. its tool icon)
without doing what it implies.

Add AutocompleteMatch::GetSuggestTemplateBehaviorKey(), built from the
template's udm and the fusebox action fields that change the click
(preselected tool, model and input source, query action override and
searchbox override), and make it part of the dedupe key. Entity
parameters, actions, entity IDs, website URIs and presentation stay out,
so e.g. an entity still dedupes with a plain search.

This generalizes kAiMode, which only handled udm=50, and is now gated
by DedupeBySuggestTemplateBehavior param.

[Blink] Add StaticAsciiStringImpl and String::FromLiteral

Introduce StaticAsciiStringImpl<N>, MakeStaticStringImpl(),
StaticStringLiteral<N>, and String::FromLiteral<Str>() to construct
compile-time ASCII StringImpl instances (with precomputed rapidhash
hashes and ASCII flags) directly in read-only .rodata without heap
allocation or reference-count traffic.

AtomicStringTable::Add() so converting a static non-atomic String to
AtomicString interns its characters instead of mutating .rodata.

Append DevTools saves to content URIs with an explicit mode
📋 Metadata
commit
6b9b0dafb0d5c6e9f55ac823fba52a46cd7a76f2
[ log ]
author
Keigo Oka <oka@google.com>
Sun Nov 08 23:42:40 2026

Sun Nov 08 23:42:40 2026
tree
7a61dd2974eafc844a0e58754159b6d8c56178db
parent
9765bb47d8bf9c144719675b7109601a62b5e6bb [ diff ] 
Append DevTools saves to content URIs with an explicit mode

DevTools streams exports (HAR, console, coverage, heap snapshots) by
saving an empty file and then appending each chunk. On Android the save
dialog returns a SAF document content URI, and DevToolsFileHelper opens
it for each chunk with FLAG_OPEN_ALWAYS | FLAG_APPEND, which the Android
adapter mapped to "wa". A later CL in this series rejects OPEN_ALWAYS
and APPEND for content URIs, because providers cannot honor
create-or-open by name, which would leave every chunked export as an
empty file.

Open content URIs with base::OpenAndroidDocument() and
AndroidDocumentAccessMode::kAppend. Save() has already created the
document, so the create-if-absent part of OPEN_ALWAYS was never needed
there, and the provider receives the same "wa" mode as before. Native
paths keep the existing flags. The existing
DevToolsFileHelperTest.AppendContentUri covers this path.

Truncate and set O_APPEND when saving MHTML to content URIs
📋 Metadata

9765bb47d8bf9c144719675b7109601a62b5e6bb
[ log ]
author
Keigo Oka <oka@google.com>
Sun Nov 08 23:41:33 2026

Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sun Nov 08 23:41:33 2026
tree
4aceff33c9f9a9bc48156dbb2952203bd320c9f7
parent
3a88a38c4315d2b817fddbc5180d856129ad216f [ diff ] 
Truncate and set O_APPEND when saving MHTML to content URIs

CreateMHTMLFile opens its output with FLAG_CREATE_ALWAYS | FLAG_APPEND |
FLAG_NO_FOLLOW so that an existing destination is truncated and the
descriptor handed to renderer processes is append-only.

On Android, Save Page passes a content:// URI selected via the system
file picker. Android document providers do not offer a combined
truncate-and-append mode string ("wa" appends without truncating, while
"wt" truncates without O_APPEND), and FLAG_NO_FOLLOW does not apply to a
descriptor returned by a ContentProvider.

Open content:// destinations with base::OpenAndroidDocument(...,
kOverwrite) ("wt") so existing contents are truncated, and set O_APPEND
on the returned descriptor via fcntl(F_SETFL) before passing it to
renderers.

Create SAF files in NativeFileUtil without exclusive-create flags
📋 Metadata
commit
3a88a38c4315d2b817fddbc5180d856129ad216f
[ log ]
author
Keigo Oka <oka@google.com>
Sun Nov 08 23:41:17 2026
committer

Sun Nov 08 23:41:17 2026
tree
3e0ed4698e40c41d4add32b38e26802c9d89335f
parent
099c2bcf25440bbe606a12bb8b2bbf1853be14c4 [ diff ] 
Create SAF files in NativeFileUtil without exclusive-create flags

NativeFileUtil::EnsureFileExists() creates a missing file by opening it
with FLAG_CREATE and treating FILE_ERROR_EXISTS as success. For a SAF
virtual document path (/SAF/...), used by DevTools workspaces and other
isolated file systems on Android, OpenAndroidFile() previously resolved
the path and, on a miss, called VirtualDocumentPath::CreateOrOpen()
before opening the descriptor. Because Android providers do not offer
atomic exclusive creation by display name, a later CL in this series
rejects FLAG_CREATE on SAF paths with FILE_ERROR_INVALID_OPERATION.

Handle SAF paths the way the content URI branch already does, with
explicit document operations: resolve the name, and call
base::CreateAndroidDocument() only when a complete provider listing
reports it absent. Lookup failures other than NOT_FOUND, such as an
incomplete listing or a permission denial, are returned instead of
triggering a create. The document is created as
"application/octet-stream": FileSystemOperation callers keep addressing
the virtual path by display name, and AOSP providers append an extension
for any other type whose extension their MIME map does not recognize,
after which that name no longer resolves. A missing parent directory
still yields FILE_ERROR_NOT_FOUND, matching the non-Android contract.

27cec857a08a0ee0e1b41f5eb18c07467a0ce093
[ log ]

tree
25131300fa459906dedb84bf592a8537b7848771
parent
0e8eddbaed72d622a021350ffd5f5737122640e5 [ diff ] 
Open download intermediate URIs with an explicit read/write mode

DownloadDocumentUriBridge::OpenDocumentUri() opened the intermediate
document with FLAG_OPEN_ALWAYS | FLAG_WRITE | FLAG_READ. The document
already exists because DownloadCollectionBridge created it, so the
create-if-absent part of OPEN_ALWAYS was never needed; the Android
adapter mapped the combination to "rw". A later CL in this series
rejects OPEN_ALWAYS for content URIs, since Android providers cannot
honor create-or-open by name, which would make every download to a
document URI fail at BaseFile initialization.

Open the URI with base::OpenAndroidDocument() and
AndroidDocumentAccessMode::kReadWrite. This asks the provider for
exactly the mode the download already used: read access so BaseFile can
hash the bytes of a resumed download, and no truncation.

817799425e44fd66137c3ccbcad031ac26df4e0e [ diff ] 
Wait for initial tab background color in SetBackgroundColorForNewTab

BrowserTest.SetBackgroundColorForNewTab navigates the first tab to a red
data: URL, immediately opens and activates a second tab, and checks that
Browser::OnActiveTabChanged copied the red background color onto the new
tab's RenderWidgetHostView.

ui_test_utils::NavigateToURL only waits for DidStopLoading(), not for
the renderer's background color update to reach the browser's
RenderWidgetHostView. When ActivateTabAt runs before that update
arrives, old_view->GetBackgroundColor() is still std::nullopt, so
CopyBackgroundColorIfPresentFrom does not copy anything and the test
fails.

https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Abrowser_tests%21gtest%3A%3ABrowserTest%23SetBackgroundColorForNewTab

Wait for the initial tab's RenderWidgetHostView::GetBackgroundColor() to
become SK_ColorRED before opening and activating the second tab.

[SettingsRefresh2026] Refactor autofill_page_v2

The autofill_page was forked to autofill_page_v2. This CL refactors the
autofill_page_v2. Since the page no longer has chips, the data hierarchy
structure was removed. Instead we have properties that track counts of
items in the pages for each of the cr-link-row of the first section (For
example, passwordManagerCount_ keeps track of the number of passwords
and passkeys, and displays that at the end of the passwords
cr-link-row).

The shopping row needs an icon and string which needs to be supplied by
UX.

screenshot: https://screenshot-v2.corp.google.com/05eta4ml2dan8

With shopping and suggestions from gemini enabled:
https://screenshot.corp.google.com/6mm298fpndn5o

Reland "Make disabled web controls translucent in dark mode"

This is a reland of commit 6892edb558fa946aad4c21a7bf7e17c604e2d4a5.

The original CL was reverted because
virtual/dark-color-scheme/fast/forms/color-scheme/search/\
search-appearance-basic.html failed deterministically on CI builder
mac15-x64-rel-tests while all try-bots were green, i.e. the platform/mac
baseline updated by the CL did not match what mac15-x64 actually
renders. Force the mac15 try-bot to run on this reland so any remaining
mac15 baselines can be captured before landing.

Original change's description:
> Make disabled web controls translucent in dark mode
>
> Disabled text fields and checkboxes painted an opaque dark gray fill in
> dark mode so the page behind them never showed through, while light mode
> uses a translucent fill over a lighten layer; mirror the light-mode
> alphas in dark mode behind a kill-switch feature.
>
> PaintButton draws through PaintLightenLayer, so the alpha change also
> affects the anti-aliased edges of disabled buttons.
>
> Fixed: 421348760
> Include-Ci-Only-Tests: true
> Change-Id: I361daae25ef85589917c1b6585c7197fe3051e92
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8676046
> Commit-Queue: Avi Drissman <avi@chromium.org>
> Reviewed-by: Mason Freed <masonf@chromium.org>
> Reviewed-by: Avi Drissman <avi@chromium.org>
> Auto-Submit: Helmut Januschka <helmut@januschka.com>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1736494}

ec7c099cadebb1b277f2b07e8c570fa761e1ff90
parent
9afe1d6562d063295016d48af3b565269777ec21 [ diff ] 
Use Desktop-allowlisted string IDs in GlicToastBrowserTest

On official Windows Chrome builds, enable_resource_allowlist_generation
strips GRIT string resources whose IDs are not referenced in production
code linked into chrome.dll. GlicToastBrowserTest passed
IDS_GLIC_CHAT_HIDDEN_TITLE, IDS_GLIC_CHAT_HIDDEN_DESCRIPTION,
IDS_GLIC_TASK_PAUSED_TITLE, and IDS_GLIC_TASK_PAUSED_DESCRIPTION, which
are only referenced in Android-only production code, causing
ui::ResourceBundle::GetLocalizedString to CHECK-crash across five
GlicToastBrowserTest cases on chrome/ci/win64:
- ShowWithTitleAndDescription
- ShowWithTitleOnly
- DestroyBeforeExpiryIsSafe
- AutoDismissSetsIsShowingFalse
- ReassignWhileShowingPreemptsAndTransitions

https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Abrowser_tests%21gtest%3A%3AGlicToastBrowserTest%23ShowWithTitleAndDescription

Replace those sample string IDs with Glic string IDs referenced in
cross-platform production code linked into chrome.dll.

Bug: None
